## Milestone 11 — Control (goal: the day adapts, the user decides)

Spec: `11-standout-roadmap.md` C1–C4.

- [x] M11.0 Week columns get Smallest step and Unstick (the M10.7 follow-up; owner, 2026-10-25: "M11's first commit"). Done 2026-10-25. One helper, `features/timeline/state/day_cues.dart` (`dayCues`), now feeds Today, the spread and Week, so they can't disagree. A Week card carries the cue as its last line in the accent (`weekCardCue`): from 48 px it replaces the time line, from 68 px it sits under it; a shorter card keeps it as a screen-reader custom action. The cue is its own tap target: Smallest step opens Focus (as Start does on Today); Unstick opens `showAppSheet` with the same `UnstickLine` and writes through `answerUnstick` (step 1 + `move_count = 0`, Undo). Rejected: opening the task editor (it can't reset the count, and would be a second Unstick flow); always showing the line (cards are sized by time, a 1 h card has 36 px). Tests: `week_cues_test` (Focus, answer writes step 1 and resets, two moves never ask, short card's action); golden `week_cues_{light,dark}_expanded` (derived).
- [x] M11.1 C1 Rebalance: triggers — Running late, a dot overrunning its end by > 10 min, an imported event moving (M7), "Shift the rest" with > 2 followers — open `ProposalSheet` instead of applying: rules first (shift → shrink optional → move past Bedtime to tomorrow; pinned untouched) from `DaySchedulerService`; per-change chips "shifted +25 min", "pinned · untouched", "shrunk to 45 min", "moved to tomorrow 9:00", per-row × to drop a change, toggles Shift followers / Allow shrinking optional / Push past bedtime, "2 h 15 free", Accept plan / Discard, "Nothing moves until you accept · Undo for 30 s after". Model only for wording, optional. **Reference** `B-ios-rebalance.png`. **Platforms** sheet on phones; panel on Mac/web. **Done when** rules tests (seeded), nothing-before-Accept test, goldens. Split (2026-10-25): this entry covers Running late, the overrun and "Shift the rest"; the imported-event trigger is M11.1b. Done 2026-10-25. Rules (`domain/scheduling/rebalance.dart`, pure): planned tasks starting at or after the delay's start; pinned tasks and events the shift may not carry (`movableBlock`) are fixed and never move. Up to the first pin, **Shift followers** on moves every task by the delay keeping its gap (off: only what collides, gaps absorb the delay); if that overruns the next fixed block it falls back to just-enough; then **Allow shrinking optional** shortens optional tasks, last first; what still doesn't fit moves past the block. At the end of the day, unless **Push past bedtime**, tasks past Bedtime go to tomorrow's first free slot from Wake Up, else the Inbox. Tasks never start earlier than planned and keep their order. "Optional" (the specs had no field; written into 11 C1): the user's own, unpinned, not started, energy not high; it may lose a quarter (rounded up to 5 min), never below 15 min. Rejected: a new `optional` column (a schema change for one rule); shrinking every task. Proposal: `domain/proposals/rebalance_proposal.dart` — shifted / "shrunk to" / moved to tomorrow or Inbox, plus "pinned · untouched" for each pin that bounded a changed stretch (not written); each written task goes through `countMove` (owner: "Rebalance counts moves when it writes"). Triggers (`features/timeline/state/rebalance_flow.dart`, `openRebalance`): the Running late sheet's Shift the rest; the late nudge's Catch up when the task ended > 10 min ago (delay = overrun rounded up to 5; ≤ 10 min still opens the sheet); the live row's and task sheet's Shift the rest when it would move > 2 tasks (≤ 2 still shifts at once, with Undo). Sheet: `ProposalRequest` gained `options` (rule chips that recompute the plan; dropped lines come back on a rule change — a new plan) and `footnote`; `ProposalThreadList` shows "N free" before Bedtime (0 for single-task proposals). `runLate` removed (replaced). The model-for-wording option is not used (rules wording is fixed copy). Fixed in passing: an unwatched day (tomorrow) was disposed mid-read — `openRebalance` holds it while loading. Tests: `rebalance_test` (13 incl. a 400-run seeded property: pins/events never move, nothing earlier, order kept, no overlaps, shrink floor, sent-off only without Push past bedtime), `rebalance_flow_test` over the real database (nothing before Accept while rules change, a line is dropped and on Discard; Accept = one batch with moves counted; a11y), Running late sheet / spread / nudge tests updated; goldens `rebalance_sheet_*` × light/dark × compact/medium/expanded × 1x/2x; proposal goldens re-baselined for the free line.
- [x] M11.1b C1 Rebalance trigger: an imported calendar event moving (M7) — the importer reports events whose start changed on today; Today shows a quiet prompt "Standup moved to 10:30 · Rebalance?" that opens `openRebalance` from the event's new end with the shift as the delay. **Done when** importer diff test, prompt widget test, nothing-before-Accept via the shared harness. Done 2026-10-25. The import plan reports `moved` (`CalendarMove`: occurrence, old and new start, old length) for a planned, visible occurrence whose start the calendar changed; the importer hands them to `onMoved` after the commit, into the session-only `calendarMovesProvider` (a second move of the same event keeps its first start; a move back drops it). Rule (`domain/scheduling/calendar_move_prompt.dart`, `moveToRebalance`): offered only for a move **later** within the same local day, not over yet, that now overlaps another planned task; the earliest wins. Rejected: prompting for every move (a move earlier frees time and Rebalance never moves tasks earlier; a move into a free stretch needs nothing). Delay start: the event's **old end**, not its new end as first written — from the new end, the tasks the move now overlaps start before the delay and would not be moved; the event itself is kept where the calendar put it (`openRebalance(keep:)`, so "Move calendar events too" can't shift it again). Prompt above the late nudge, below the Day review; either button puts it away; the sheet is titled "Calendar changed". Tests: plan (move reported with old/new start; a rename, a done event or no change reports nothing), importer (reported after the commit), rule (later + collision only; earlier, free, done, over, other day, stale, gone; earliest wins), prompt over a real database (asks once, Not now, Rebalance opens the plan, nothing before Accept via the shared harness, a11y).
- [x] M11.2 C3 Reality thread: a Plan / Reality toggle in the day card header; Reality draws the planned thread as a ghost lane beside the actual lane (B1), captions "7:42 – 8:16 · planned 7:30", "took 34 min", "not started yet", "usually 2 h 20", and the insight strip ("Deep work usually takes you 2 h 20 · Add will …", OK). **Reference** `B-ios-reality-thread.png`. **Platforms** phones in the DayPager card; Mac/web in the spread's today card. **Done when** ghost-thread golden (the milestone gate), DST day, a dot with no actuals. Done 2026-10-25. Split: the "usually 2 h 20" caption and the insight strip need M11.3's learning, so they move to M11.3 (rejected: a placeholder strip with nothing to say). Rules (`domain/scheduling/reality.dart`, `realityOf`): a task with an actual start and end is drawn where it ran ("took N"); started and not ticked, from its start to now (at least its planned length, so it reads live); the next planned task with no start, and any whose planned start passed without one, say "not started yet"; everything else (no actuals, done or skipped without a start, ticked done with only an end) stays where it was planned — never inferred (B1). - [x] M11.3 C3 learning (local, rule-based): per title, median actual duration and median start offset over the last 10 occurrences; used by Add (duration chip pre-selected, "usually 2 h 20"), the ThreadPicker caption ("you tend to start this ~15 min after plan") and M11.1. **Done when** learning tests on synthetic history (outliers, < 3 samples → nothing, renamed titles). Also from M11.2: the Reality thread's "usually 2 h 20" caption on the live row and the insight strip ("Deep work usually takes you 2 h 20 · Add will …", OK). Done 2026-10-25. Rules (`domain/scheduling/learning.dart`, `LearningIndex`): only runs with a real actual start and end (B1; 1–1440 min, deleted rows skipped); per task the last 10 by actual start, matched on the normalised title **or the same series**, so a renamed repeat keeps its history (a renamed one-off is a new task); under 3 samples → nothing (amended by M13.1: 4, `LearningThresholds.taskMinSamples`, docs/13 §2); median length rounded to 5 min, median start offset (actual − planned) in minutes — the median keeps one outlier in ten from moving it. Source: `learningIndexProvider` over the last 90 days (local only). Place (a `ProposalSheet`) / Leave in Inbox; share image (titles off by default) via `share_plus`; "No streaks here — just what happened." Sunday at Bedtime or on demand. **Reference** `B-ios-week-review.png`. **Platforms** sheet on phones; panel on Mac/web; share uses the OS sheet (web: download/Web Share). **Done when** goldens, share image golden with titles off/on, sentence rules tested, no-shame audit. Done 2026-10-25. Rules (`domain/review/week_review.dart`): the week is the one (from the user's first weekday) holding today, or on a week's first day the one that just ended — so Sunday at Bedtime shows the week ending tonight (weeks from Monday) or the 18 – 24 Oct of the render (weeks from Sunday); each day is `DaySnapshot.ofDay` (B5, every timed non-skipped task Wake Up → Bedtime, derived on read; no table); dots sized by planned minutes (square-root, so area follows hours), ring segments by task length, done solid / not done at the upcoming alpha; Busiest = most planned (ties: earlier day), Lightest = least among planned days, none when only one day had anything. The sentence (owner, 2026-10-25: nothing beyond the two insight lines, no comparison with another week, no count of days with something left): first half from real starts (B1) — "Mornings mostly went to plan." when ≥ 3 morning tasks really started and ≥ 80 % of them within 15 min of plan, else the same for afternoons, else "Everything you planned got done.", or "A quiet week: nothing was planned."; second half "Thursday carried the most; Saturday breathed." (rejected: the render's "all week", which the rule can't promise). Entry points: Today ⋯ "This week"; at Bedtime on the week's last day a prompt after the Day review. Sheet on phones, popover from the medium width (rejected: the right-hand panel — Place opens its proposal there). "No streaks here — just what happened." is the one string allowed to name streaks (`no_shame_copy_test` exception, it promises there are none). The reference named in the owner's note, `B-ios-week-review-alt1-dots`, is the chosen "seven dots" design filed as `B-ios-week-review.png`. Today (`features/timeline/low_energy/`): header "WED, OCT 21 · LOW-ENERGY" with the moon, "Take it easy, Kody"; the next dot = the one under way, else the next planned not-ended task (`LowEnergyDay`), as Focus's ring with "until 11:00 AM · 1 h 38 left" or "at 2:00 PM", Done (with Undo) and Not today (a one-off moves to tomorrow at the same time, a repeat or event is skipped today; with Undo — the user's own choice, so no proposal); "LATER · 9" as dots, ⌄ opens rows with times; "Move everything optional to tomorrow?" Counts hidden (no strip, no "2 of 13", spread subtitle says only the date). E2E: Android — `tool/share_capture_check.sh` sends a real ACTION_SEND with adb to a cleared app on the API 37 emulator, `integration_test/share_capture_verify_test.dart` finds the Inbox task with its note: SHARE_CAPTURE ok; iOS — Safari › Share › Dottoday › Post on the iPhone 17 Pro simulator wrote the App Group entry, and the same verify test drained a queued share into the Inbox: SHARE_CAPTURE ok. ## Milestone 14 — Voice + AI clarity (goal: speak a brain dump or a note on the device, and always know which path plans for you and how to get the better one)

Spec: `11-standout-roadmap.md` §0 B4, B6, B7 (added by M14.0); ADR-0009, ADR-0014; `10-ui-concept-a.md` §3, §5; `06-security.md` §6, §9. Owner decisions D1–D6 (owner, 2026-10-28) are recorded in ADR-0014 and below; they are not must-ask items. Still must-ask: a new pub dependency, any path where speech or task text leaves the device, a sixth App Intent or Siri on Mac/Android, removing/merging a screen, a new onboarding step, a nav change, a native target, anything on the hosted Supabase project (none needed: no schema change; the "don't show again" flags are device-local).

Owner decisions (owner, 2026-10-28):
- **D1** Speech is on-device only, through each platform's own recognizer over platform channels. No new pub dependency (no `speech_to_text`, no `permission_handler`). Audio and transcripts never leave the device, are never stored, never logged.
- **D2** The mic shows only where on-device recognition is available for the current locale; elsewhere it is hidden and keyboard dictation still works. **Approved platform exception to R-13** (Android < API 31 or no on-device recognizer, browsers without on-device Web Speech, a locale without an on-device model). Follow-up below: revisit when the platform ships on-device speech.
- **D3** Siri work is iPhone only; exactly the five App Intents (11 §0 B6), no new intent; the work is discoverability.
- **D4** AI enablement nudges: no-shame copy, never a modal, never on every use, dismissible, silent when the device cannot run the model (only the plain "simple rules" label shows).
- **D5** Permission copy — Microphone: "Dottoday listens only while the mic button is on, to turn your words into text on this device." Speech recognition: "Your words are turned into text on this device. Nothing is sent to a server." Android RECORD_AUDIO rationale in-app via ARB, same meaning.
- **D6** Brain dump dictation: a pause of ~1.5 s (`kDictationPause`, domain) or the spoken command word starts a new line — en "next", de "weiter", fr "suivant", es "siguiente" (ARB). Notes dictation has no commands and no pause-newline: text at the cursor, punctuation as the recognizer gives it.

Done 2026-10-29. As built: port `domain/speech/speech_recognizer.dart` (`SpeechAvailability`, sealed `SpeechEvent` = partial / final / stopped / error); failures are typed in the one `AppFailure` library — `SpeechFailure(SpeechFailureKind.unavailable|interrupted|recognizer)` and `PermissionFailure(AppPermission.microphone)` (a sealed `AppFailure` can only be extended in its own library, so `SpeechFailure` is a kind enum like `NetworkFailure`; rejected: a second sealed hierarchy outside `AppFailure`). `domain/speech/dictation_text.dart`: immutable `Dictation` (`start`, `partial`, `finalResult`, `tick`; explicit `now` from the injected `Clock`): words merge at the cursor by count, so a recognizer resending the whole utterance never duplicates; Brain dump: a pause of `kDictationPause` (1.5 s) or the command word settled for `kCommandSettle` (0.7 s) — or an utterance ending on it — ends the line, the word removed, never an empty line; each line starts with a capital; Plain: insert only. The rule and its examples are in the doc comment. Data: `speech_wire.dart` (words), `channel_speech_transport.dart` (`ChannelSpeechRecognizer`), `web_speech_recognizer.dart` → `web/speech_web.dart` / stub, `speech_providers.dart` (`speechRecognizerProvider`, `speechAvailabilityProvider(localeTag)`, re-read on resume). Native: `ios/Runner/SpeechBridge.swift` in both Runners (pbxproj refs; macOS path `../../ios/Runner/SpeechBridge.swift`), registered in both AppDelegates; the recognizer locale is resolved natively (the device's own region when it speaks the language, else Apple's first supported one); iOS 17 `AVAudioApplication` with the iOS 16 fallback; `addsPunctuation` from iOS 16 / macOS 13; stop ends the audio and waits ≤ 1 s for the final; stops on background (Mac: on resign active) and on audio-session interruption. `android/.../speech/SpeechBridge.kt`: API 31+, `isOnDeviceRecognitionAvailable`, API 33+ also `checkRecognitionSupport` for an installed language; permission words from `checkSelfPermission` + a remembered "asked" flag + `shouldShowRequestPermissionRationale`; restarts after each utterance and on silence until stopped; stops in `MainActivity.onPause`; permission result routed from `onRequestPermissionsResult`. Config: Info.plist mic + speech texts (D5) on iOS and macOS, de/fr/es `InfoPlist.strings`, macOS `device.audio-input` (Debug + Release), Android `RECORD_AUDIO` + `<queries>` `android.speech.RecognitionService`. Strings for M14.4–M14.5 (command word per language, mic labels, rationale, captions, tip) added here in en/de/fr/es. ---

Found on the M14 PR's CI (2026-10-29), fixed on the branch: (1) a stale `dictation_controller.g.dart` hash — the controller was edited after its last codegen; (2) `build (ios)` failed on iOS 16 APIs in `LiveActivityViews.swift` because M14.7's `xcodebuild` signing attempts let Xcode "upgrade" `ios/Runner.xcodeproj` (`LastUpgradeCheck`, extensions' `IPHONEOS_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET` 16.0 → the recommended default) and that was committed — the file is main's again plus the four `SpeechBridge.swift` lines; lesson: never run `xcodebuild` on the checked-in project without reverting what it rewrites; (3) `a11y-web` crashed ("Target crashed" opening Brain dump): `SpeechRecognition.available({processLocally: true})` kills the tab in a Chromium without Google's speech component — the web recognizer now probes only in Google Chrome and Microsoft Edge (ADR-0014), `speech_web_test` covers headless and Brave as unavailable; `popover_focus_check` and `keyboard_check` pass locally on the rebuilt web app.

- [x] Dictation stalled after a line break (owner's device check, 2026-10-30): the first line typed well, but after "next" or a pause new words hardly appeared. Cause: `Dictation` remembered the words of an utterance already used **by count** and showed only words past it; iOS starts its transcript over after a pause, so the next guess ("buy milk") was shorter than the count and was dropped until more words were said than the previous line had — and their first words were lost. Also, when Apple ended a recognition task after a silence, `SpeechBridge.swift` ended the whole session. Fix: `Dictation` keeps the used words themselves and treats a guess that no longer begins with them (normalised; one revised word allowed once three are said) as a new utterance; in Notes, words still unsettled when such a restart comes after a pause are kept first. The Swift bridge starts a new request and task on the running audio whenever Apple ends one with the mic still on (three error endings in a row with nothing heard end the session, so it never spins). Android asks for a 4 s silence before ending an utterance, so a short pause no longer restarts the recognizer and loses the first words after it (on-device recognizers may ignore the hint). Tests: 8 new `dictation_text` cases (5 fail on the old logic), a Brain dump widget test replaying the restart; iOS-sim, macOS and apk builds pass. Re-check on the devices (08 §6.1 E "Dictation on real hardware").

## Milestone 17 — Wake Up (goal: the Wake Up time rings like an alarm when the user asks for it, the morning opens on the day, and the night between Wind Down and Wake Up is planned and visible)

Spec: `15-wake-up.md` (with the owner's decisions of 2026-10-31 in §13); audit `docs/audits/m17-wake-up-inventory.md`; ADR-0017 (written with W1b). Rules:

- **Nothing changes until opted in:** nothing rings or moves differently until the user opts in (§11).
- **No-shame:** every string passes `no_shame_copy` (the M17 words added with the first strings that need them).
- **Minimal mode:** every surface has a minimal-mode variant through `LayoutPolicy`.
- **Platforms:** W1–W3 and W5 are mobile only; W4 + W6 apply on every platform.
- **Delivery:** one PR per piece, each from `origin/main`, with a screenshot sweep before each close. Version 1.2.0.

- [x] M17.0 Audit + docs: inventory, doc 15 renumbered and the owner's decisions recorded, doc 14 sleep-block row fixed, Play full-screen-intent declaration in 08 §6.1 C. Branch `docs/wake-up-audit`.
- [x] M17.1 W1a Wake Up alarm on the existing path (`feat/wake-up-w1`, PR #44):
  - **Setting:** `Settings.wakeUpAlarm` (synced, default off), with a row in Settings › Day shape and an onboarding offer that defaults to on for new users.

## Follow-ups after M12 (found at the M12.6 ship-check)

- [x] Web: a popover's autofocused text field (Add, Brain dump) shows a caret but takes keys only after a click, in the browser pane's Chromium and headless Chromium; on phones and the Mac it types at once. Check real desktop Chrome (08 §6.1 E), then hand the field DOM focus when the popover opens. The web sweep clicks first and notes it. Done 2026-10-26. Cause (traced in the browser): Brain dump opens from Today's ⋯ menu; with semantics on, the menu item's DOM element holds the browser focus and goes away as the menu closes, so the engine reports the Flutter view unfocused. When the popover's field then takes DOM focus, the view counts as refocused and the framework (`_ViewState.didChangeViewFocus`) moves Flutter's focus to the first traversable node in the view — the app-wide key catcher in `AppCommandScope` — so the field lost focus for a moment, and the engine's deferred blur of the closed connection then left the browser's focus on `flutter-view` with the caret still drawn. Add from a button never lost the view's focus, which is why it typed. Fix: the key catcher is `skipTraversal: true` (no Tab stop, like `TodayKeys`), so the framework falls back to the focused route — the popover — and the field keeps its focus; nothing is done per field (rejected: a web-only DOM refocus, deferring sheets until a menu closes — tried, still failed — and the menu on the root navigator — tried, still failed). Tests: `test/core/shortcuts/app_command_scope_focus_test.dart` replays the view's unfocus/refocus with a sheet (compact) and a popover (expanded) open — fails without the fix; `tool/a11y/popover_focus_check.mjs` (CI a11y-web) opens Add and Brain dump at 390 and 1280 and types without a click — fails on the old build (`<flutter-view>`), passes now; the keyboard checks still pass. The check also times each field, from the tap to the field's focus, and fails past 3 s (owner, 2026-10-26: slower focus on a phone browser is a bug, not a slow runner); the times are printed in CI. The web sweep types into the brain dump at both widths again.
- [x] Mac sweep for M12 after the owner answered the Mac prompts (2026-10-26): `app/tool/mac_sweep.sh` light and dark PASS, 33 screens each, attached to PR #11 (`sweep/m12-mac`, screenshots only). The first run failed in the sweep test, not the app: on the wide Mac layout `find.byType(TextField).first` typed the brain dump into the inbox's field (and after one run, Earlier dumps adds a search field), so Plan it stayed off; it now finds the field by its hint.

- [x] Week strip at 200 % text: the weekday label wraps mid-word ("We / d", "Mo / n") on a 390 px phone (seen in the M13.6 `today_learning_caption_*_t2` goldens; it predates M13). Doc 10 §5.4 says nothing truncates; fit the label (a shorter weekday form at large text, or let the strip scroll) and add a 2.0-text golden for the strip. Done 2026-10-30. When any day's short name ("Wed") doesn't fit its column on one line, **the whole week** shows the narrow form (`DateFormat('EEEEE')`: S M T W T F S), never a mix; the column's screen-reader label still says the whole day. Measured with `core/utils/text_fit.dart`, not by a text-scale threshold, so a long name in another language is covered too. The `today_learning_caption_*_t2` and `today_reality_highlight_*_t2` goldens are re-baselined; `week_strip_test` covers 100 % and 200 %.

## Task icon library (owner request, 2026-10-27)

- [x] Task icon library: Structured's icon names, in categories, plus Dottoday's own (ADR-0013, docs/10 §3). **Files** `assets/design/task_icons.json` (the source), `tool/icons/import_task_icons.py` (Lucide 0.460 / Tabler 3.24 → JSON), `tool/gen_task_icons.dart` → `core/theme/task_icons.gen.dart` (`TaskIcon`, `TaskIconCategory`), `shared/pickers/task_icon_names.gen.dart`, the `taskIcon*` ARB keys (en/de/es/fr); `core/theme/svg_path.dart` (`S`/`T`); `shared/pickers/task_icon_{choice,grid,picker}.dart` (full-height picker: colours, search, lazy category sections; the Search filter uses it too); `core/theme/icon_licenses.dart` + `assets/licenses/` (About › Licenses). **Tests** `task_icons_test` (grid bounds for every drawing, sampled along the path; Concept A keys; legacy keys; search; generator freshness, ARB merge, malformed input), picker tests in `colour_icon_block_test` (categories, search by name/category/key, no match, selection, a11y light/dark), goldens `task_icon_library_*`, `task_icon_picker_*` (light/dark × compact/medium/expanded × 1×/2× + 2.0 text, RTL), `dot_icons_*` re-baselined. Done 2026-10-27. As built: 524 icons in 16 categories. 487 of Structured's 632 listed names from Lucide/Tabler, 39 brand logos left out, 15 drawn here (broom, mop, laundry basket, oven, sink, dishwasher, frying pan, watering can, hairdryer, lawn mower, toothbrush, dancing, trousers, dress, hat), and a first **Planning & rhythm** category of 22 Dottoday-only icons (focus block, deep work, plan the day, time block, buffer, anytime, break, morning/evening routine, wind down, power nap, week review, inbox zero, habit, errands, low/high energy, screen-free, stand-up, self-care, meal prep, pay bills). Concept A's 26 keys are unchanged (no data migration); their labels now use Structured's names, and run/travel/list use the library drawing for that name. The quick icons in `ColourIconBlock` are unchanged. Owner rule (docs/10 no-shame): fr "Chess" is "Échiquier" ("Échecs" also means failures). Rejected: SF Symbols (Apple-only license), an icon font or a third-party icon package. l10n parity: icon names that read as in English come from the JSON rather than the hand-kept allow-list.

## Event-driven, no polling (owner request, 2026-10-31)

- [x] Replace every polling loop in the open app with the event that makes it needed (docs/04 §4 "Triggers"; R-08). **Sync**: the 5-minute `Timer.periodic` is gone; Realtime nudges on every change, on every channel join (catch-up after a drop) and when the server's change feed goes live (a change in the ~0.2 s before that is otherwise never announced), a broken channel is replaced with back-off (`data/sync/realtime_nudges.dart`), a failed run is retried with back-off until one succeeds (`EngineSyncService`). **Housekeeping**: the 6-hour `Timer.periodic` is gone; one timer for the moment it is next due (`domain/time/housekeeping_due.dart`: local midnight or the daily backup, `BackupService.nextDailyDueUtc`), re-armed after each run, one run at a time; the OS's `wake`/`clock` events run it (`data/system/system_events.dart` over `dottoday/system_events`: `SystemEventsBridge` in the iOS and macOS AppDelegate, `system/SystemEvents.kt` on Android). **Calendars**: device calendar changes (EventKit, `CalendarContract` observer, started once access is granted) import device events after a 2 s settle (`CalendarActions.deviceCalendarChanged`). No fallback timer is kept. **Tests** `tool/sync/realtime_live_check_test.dart` against the local stack (join, a change at once after joining, a change, a real socket drop with a change while down, a closed channel; each nudged within ~1 s), device `calendar_live_change` on the iPhone 17 Pro simulator and the API 37 emulator (added / moved / deleted with no resume, each ~2.1–2.3 s), `realtime_nudges_test`, `engine_sync_service_test` (retry until success, then stop), `housekeeping_due_test` (DST, month/year ends, overdue backup), `backup_service_test` (next due), `app_lifecycle_test` (nothing before it is due; midnight; backup first; wake/clock; coalesced events; calendar burst → one import). Kept by design: the OS's daily background refresh on iOS/Android, timers for known moments, Realtime's keep-alive.
- [ ] Google/Microsoft calendar change webhooks (Google push channels, Microsoft Graph subscriptions) into the backend, so cloud calendars update while the app is open on the web too. Owner gate: hosted Supabase project and the providers' consoles (CLAUDE.md §10).

## Pricing (owner sign-off pending)

The free/Pro split in `11-standout-roadmap.md` is a **draft** the owner will review. Until it is signed off, everything is built ungated; the only pricing code is `domain/entitlements` (M6.5.11) with one `isPro` flag, `true` in every build. No paywall, no purchase flow, no store products. Features may read `isPro` but no build sets it false.
